Corrected live census on 2026-10-07: 153 listed stores (DE 78, NL 22, BE 15, PL 12, CZ 10, AT 6, LU 6, HU 4), of which about 150 are actually trading. In the window from the deal close to now there were about 14-17 gross closures and 2-3 openings, a net of about -11 to -15 on about 165 stores (-7% to -9% in 13 months, roughly 6.5-8% a year). That compares with a benchmark of about 4% a year for Europe as a whole (581 to 559) and a flat Bloomberg consensus. At least 10-12 of the closures carry post-FYE (after 2026-01-31) evidence, which works out to at least about 9% a year gross in this sample.
